Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the genomics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 914. The moduli space has dimension at most 855 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on genomics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 914. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 855. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any genomics module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 602. The projective resolution has length at most 562 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the genomics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^602 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 562-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
